voltara_ruins_lobby_select = {
 scope = title
 is_shown = { is_target_in_global_variable_list = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this } }
 effect = {
  if = { limit = { is_target_in_global_variable_list = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this } }
   remove_list_global_variable = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this }
   every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = { limit = { tier >= tier_county }
    remove_list_global_variable = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this }
   }
   empire ?= { remove_list_global_variable = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this } }
   kingdom ?= { remove_list_global_variable = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this } }
   duchy ?= { remove_list_global_variable = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this } }
  }
  else = {
   add_to_global_variable_list = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this }
   every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = { limit = { tier >= tier_county NOT = { is_target_in_global_variable_list = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this } } }
    add_to_global_variable_list = { name = voltara_selected_ruins_titles target = this }
   }
  }
 }
}
voltara_ruins_lobby_block_settlement = {
 scope = character
 is_shown = { exists = global_var:voltara_wilderness_uncolonisable }
 effect = {
  if = { limit = { exists = global_var:voltara_wilderness_uncolonisable } remove_global_variable = voltara_wilderness_uncolonisable }
  else = { set_global_variable = { name = voltara_wilderness_uncolonisable value = yes } }
 }
}
voltara_ruins_lobby_wasteland = {
 scope = character
 is_valid = { any_in_global_list = { variable = voltara_selected_ruins_titles tier = tier_county } }
 effect = {
  save_scope_value_as = { name = ruins_mode value = flag:wasteland }
  voltara_ensure_ruins_keeper = yes
  every_in_global_list = { variable = voltara_selected_ruins_titles limit = { tier = tier_county }
   voltara_convert_ruin_county = yes
   if = { limit = { exists = global_var:voltara_wilderness_uncolonisable } set_variable = { name = voltara_uncolonisable value = yes } }
  }
  global_var:voltara_ruins_keeper = { every_character_war = { end_war = white_peace } }
  voltara_clear_ruins_selection = yes
 }
}
voltara_ruins_lobby_ruin = {
 scope = character
 is_valid = { any_in_global_list = { variable = voltara_selected_ruins_titles tier = tier_county } }
 effect = {
  save_scope_value_as = { name = ruins_mode value = flag:ruin }
  voltara_ensure_ruins_keeper = yes
  every_in_global_list = { variable = voltara_selected_ruins_titles limit = { tier = tier_county }
   voltara_convert_ruin_county = yes
   if = { limit = { exists = global_var:voltara_wilderness_uncolonisable } set_variable = { name = voltara_uncolonisable value = yes } }
  }
  global_var:voltara_ruins_keeper = { every_character_war = { end_war = white_peace } }
  voltara_clear_ruins_selection = yes
 }
}
